**Trigger:** Any incident requiring the RUNBOOK rollback after 07-14.

**Consequence:** During an incident, an operator following RUNBOOK:92 looks up the last deploy tag and finds one from 2026-07-14. Checking it out rolls back 96 commits — every R16/R17/R19/R20/R21/R22/R23 fix and all of W1-W6 (exit outbox, CAS-gated fills, fail-closed range gate, range_spec authority, the corrected engine cost model, observe mode) — reinstating the very live-money defects those rounds were bought with, at the worst possible moment. The RUNBOOK's own precondition ('TAG every verified deploy going forward') has been unmet for ~7 weeks and nothing detects it.

**Suggested fix:** Tag the current verified deploy now (`git tag -a deploy-20260901 -m 'smoke pass + first-RTH liveness OK' 43d0409`), and make the omission self-detecting: have scripts/smoke_deploy.py (or check_first_rth_liveness.py) run `git describe --tags --exact-match HEAD` and print a loud WARN — or fail the smoke — when HEAD carries no deploy-* tag, so the deploy checklist cannot silently skip the step again.

## docs-policy-10  [doc_vs_code | ops_reporting | live_path=False]

**Where:** docs/RUNBOOK.md (entire file: no dead-man section, no healthchecks alert->action row); scripts/verify_deadman.py:8-12 (the only arming steps); keeper_ait.bat:49-70; PLAN.md W6 entry (bot-day-02)

**Claim:** The RUNBOOK — the operator's 'one page: what each alert means and what YOU do' — has no entry for the dead-man channel at all: no arming steps, no row for a healthchecks.io 'check is down' notification, and no statement that a green external monitor now requires bot liveness (W6). This is the one channel that survives machine death, which is exactly what happened on 09-02.

**Evidence:** grep -i 'deadman|dead-man|healthchecks' docs/RUNBOOK.md -> no matches (the alert->action table lines 15-32 covers Telegram messages only). Arming steps exist only in verify_deadman.py's docstring ('Create a free check at https://healthchecks.io (Period 5 min, Grace 15 min) ... Save it as the ONLY line of data/deadman_url.txt'). data/deadman_url.txt present (Aug 11). keeper.log 09-02 shows the keeper (the pinger) was not running for the whole session.

**Trigger:** Operator receives a healthchecks 'down' email/notification and consults the RUNBOOK.

**Consequence:** An armed external monitor can page the operator ('check is down' email/push) with zero documented meaning or action, and the one incident class it exists for — whole-machine death with live positions, which happened for the entire 09-02 session — is the class where every other channel (Telegram, dashboard, logs) is also dead. The operator must reverse-engineer the response from a script docstring and a .bat comment; the W6 semantic change (green now asserts the bot is trading, /fail is sent when it is not) is recorded nowhere an operator reads.

**Suggested fix:** Add a RUNBOOK row: 'healthchecks.io DOWN / FAIL -> machine, keeper or bot is dead, or the bot is not demonstrably alive in RTH (W6 gates the ping on ops_health liveness): check the machine is up and logged in, then `python scripts/verify_deadman.py --no-ping`, then the BOT DOWN row.' Move the four arming steps out of verify_deadman.py's docstring into the RUNBOOK, and add a deadman line to the quarterly-drill table (unplug test) so the channel gets exercised like the kill switch and the backups.

## hardcoded-live-03-commission-estimate-ignores-w5-config-homes  [hardcoded | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/execution/executor.py:2433-2446 (commission_estimate, 0.65 literal), :2422-2428 (fill-time subtraction); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:768-783 (fill-time true-up gate), :3381-3396 (post-market true-up; marker deleted unconditionally at :3396), :2862 (partial-exit `partial_pnl -= 0.65 * qty_to_close`); config.yaml:171-179 + src/ait/config/settings.py:244-268 (backtest.commission_per_contract 0.65, regulatory_fees_per_contract 0.2668 — W5 config homes); executor already holds settings: orchestrator.py:294-295, executor.py:213-234

**Claim:** The live per-trade commission estimate is a hardcoded 0.65/contract/side while W5 gave the measured all-in cost a config home (commission_per_contract + regulatory_fees_per_contract = 0.9168/leg, matching the live ledger); the executor never reads it, so live and research now disagree on friction by $2.13 per condor round trip, and the flat estimate stays in realized_pnl permanently whenever the executions ledger carries no attributed commission because the post-market path only trues up when _real > 0 yet deletes the trueup_pending marker regardless.

**Evidence:** Executed probe: TradeExecutor.commission_estimate(iron_condor, qty 1) = 5.2; config (0.65+0.2668) x 4 legs x 2 sides = 7.3344; gap 2.1344. Ledger probe (mode=ro): 94 non-BAG execution rows with commission>0: mean 0.9089/contract-leg, min 0.6195, max 1.0586. DB: verdict-sample row T-20260707-093601-46bb60 SPY iron_condor closed realized_pnl 5.80, trades.commission 0.0, ledger_comm 0.0, n_exec 0 — it carries the flat $5.20 estimate (a 4-leg round trip at the measured 0.9089/leg is ~$7.27). Code: executor.py:2446 `return 0.65 * legs_per_side * qty * 2  # entry + exit`; executor.py:2422 comment '~$0.65/contract/side (IBKR tiered pricing)'; orchestrator.py:3385 `if _t is not None and _real > 0 and _t.realized_pnl is not None:` ... :3396 `self._state.delete_state(f"trueup_pending_{_tid}")` executed outside that if.

**Trigger:** Any close whose executions have no commission attribution (entry legs swept under an orderId/permId map lost to a restart before the sweep, pre-ledger rows, commissionReport never arriving, trade_id '' rows) — realized_pnl keeps 0.65-flat ($5.20/condor) instead of the measured ~$7.3; every such row feeds the go-live PF/scorecard.

**Consequence:** Any close whose executions carry no attributed commission (pre-ledger rows, commissionReport never arriving, orderId/permId map lost to a restart) keeps a $5.20 flat estimate instead of the config-homed ~$7.33 all-in — $2.13/contract optimistic on that verdict-sample row (one such IC row exists today, T-20260707-093601-46bb60), and live and research now disagree on the friction number for the same quantity even though W5 gave it one home.

**Suggested fix:** Make the estimate resolve (backtest.commission_per_contract + backtest.regulatory_fees_per_contract) from the settings the executor already holds (instance method with a module-level resolver via resolve_config_value for the class-qualified callers at orchestrator.py:770/:3387), apply the same at orchestrator.py:2862, and pin with an executing test that commission_estimate(iron_condor, qty 1) == (c+f)x4x2 from a loaded Settings. Pair it with the already-registered fix: keep and log the trueup_pending marker when _real == 0.

## hardcoded-live-04-env-only-trading-gates-outside-contract  [hardcoded | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/strategies/iron_condor.py:144 (AIT_IRON_CONDOR_IV_FLOOR "15"), :189-190 (AIT_IC_DELTA_MIN/MAX "0.15"/"0.30"), :210-211 (AIT_IC_EM_MIN/MAX "0.6"/"1.3"), :169,177 (short delta 0.20 literal), :97 (IV last-resort 0.20); src/ait/strategies/base.py:192 (tolerance=0.07 default); src/ait/risk/manager.py:435 (AIT_IV_RANK_CAP "85"), :470 (AIT_MAX_PER_EXPIRY "3"); src/ait/execution/executor.py:36 (AIT_ENTRY_ORDER_TIMEOUT "240"), :741 (AIT_ENTRY_LADDER_START "0.25"); src/ait/execution/portfolio.py:569 (AIT_CREDIT_TOUCH_STOP "1" — already registered live-bt-mirrors-03); src/ait/config/runtime_env.py:72-88 (CONTRACT_DEFAULTS has none of these); tests/test_r19_config_authority.py:77-80 (scan regex limited to CONTRACT_DEFAULTS keys); .env (only AIT_LIQ_MAX_SPREAD/MIN_OI/MIN_VOLUME set); run_orchestrator.py (sets no AIT_ vars)

**Claim:** Nine live trading gates are read from env with inline literal defaults that have no config.yaml home and are not in CONTRACT_DEFAULTS, so the R19 'no private fallback' guard cannot see them, and because neither .env nor the launcher sets any of them the inline literals ARE the operating values — the 17-private-fallback class R19 closed, recurring one layer out. Adjacent new defect: the short-strike delta selection is governed by two different literal windows — _find_strike_by_delta tolerance 0.07 around 0.20 (admits [0.13,0.27]) and the env band [0.15,0.30] — whose intersection [0.15,0.27] is what actually trades and is documented nowhere.

**Evidence:** grep over src/ait/{bot,execution,risk,strategies,data}: environ.get sites listed in `where`; CONTRACT_DEFAULTS keys: KMP/OMP, AIT_MARKET_DATA_TYPE, AIT_IC_WING_K, AIT_IC_MIN_CREDIT_WIDTH, AIT_IC_MIN_CREDIT, AIT_CREDIT_LOSS_LIMIT, AIT_ALLOW_UNDEFINED_RISK, AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS only. test_r19_config_authority.py:79-80 `r'environ\.get\(\s*"(' + "|".join(k for k in CONTRACT_DEFAULTS if k.startswith("AIT_")) + r')"'`. Executed probe with a single-contract chain: delta 0.14 -> tolerance(0.07) admits=True, band[0.15,0.30] admits=False; delta 0.28 -> tolerance admits=False, band admits=True. .env AIT_ key names: AIT_LIQ_MAX_SPREAD AIT_LIQ_MIN_OI AIT_LIQ_MIN_VOLUME; run_orchestrator.py: no AIT_ assignments.

**Trigger:** unjustified: no config rationale anywhere (values live only in code comments); an operator cannot tune strike placement, IV-rank floor/cap, per-expiry cap, order timeout or ladder start through config.yaml, and a future edit to either delta literal silently changes the effective [0.15,0.27] window.

**Consequence:** config.yaml, declared by user policy (R19c) to be the only place trading values live, is silent on nine live entry/execution gates: IV-rank floor 15 and cap 85, short-strike delta band [0.15,0.30] (effectively [0.15,0.27] once base.py's 0.07 tolerance intersects it), EM band [0.6,1.3], per-expiry cap 3, entry order timeout 240s, ladder start 0.25. An operator reading config.yaml gets a false picture of what admits a condor, none of these can be tuned without editing code or discovering undocumented env names, and the R19 no-private-fallback scan cannot see them because they were never contract keys.

**Suggested fix:** Promote each to CONTRACT_DEFAULTS + CONFIG_BACKED with config.yaml/settings homes (options.ic_delta_band, options.ic_em_band, risk.iv_rank_floor, risk.iv_rank_cap, risk.max_per_expiry, execution.entry_order_timeout_s, execution.ladder_start_frac) read via contract_float/contract_str; derive _find_strike_by_delta's tolerance from the band (or pass tolerance=None and let the band be the single gate); extend the R19 scan to fail on ANY environ.get("AIT_...", "<literal>") outside runtime_env.py (allowlist the documented exemptions); executing tests that each gate reads the config value.

**Claim:** Three config.yaml backtest values that R19c declares the operating source are read by nothing on the walk-forward / nightly / CLI / dashboard paths and are contradicted by code literals: config says 3 concurrent positions, patience 20 and $100k; the nightly and CLI run 1 position, patience 0 and $50k. Only scripts/run_integration_test.py reads optimize_patience; no file reads settings.backtest.max_concurrent_positions at all. The dashboard export additionally records patience 20 and max_concurrent 1 as the run's config regardless of what ran.

**Evidence:** Executed (Python313): 'config backtest.max_concurrent_positions = 3 | WalkForwardConfig() = 1 | optimizer default = 1'; 'config backtest.optimize_patience = 20 | WalkForwardConfig() = 0 | optimizer default = 0'. Reader grep over src/, scripts/, run_*.py for '.backtest.max_concurrent_positions|.backtest.optimize_patience': 0 hits outside settings.py except scripts/run_integration_test.py:596 (patience only). walkforward.py:105 'max_concurrent_positions: int = 1', :109 'optimize_patience: int = 0       # 0 = disabled'. master.py:769 passes '--capital', '50000' while config.yaml:117 initial_capital: 100000 is commented 'Starting capital for walk-forward / integration tests'. export.py:297-298 literal '"max_concurrent_positions": 1', '"optimize_patience": 20'. Register grep 'max_concurrent_positions|optimize_patience': 0 hits.

**Trigger:** Every run of run_backtest.py / the nightly health backtest / the dashboard walk-forward page: the operator's declared 3-position book is simulated as a 1-position book (live runs up to 10 open / 6 credit), and the early-stop the config declares never engages.

**Consequence:** config.yaml declares a 3-position research book and 20-trial early stop that no walk-forward, CLI, weekly health run or dashboard path can ever apply (only run_integration_test honours patience), while the R19 divergence report implies config governs them; an operator editing backtest.max_concurrent_positions changes nothing, and run archives record max_concurrent 1 / patience 20 regardless of what ran.

**Suggested fix:** Either (a) make WalkForwardConfig.max_concurrent_positions / optimize_patience None-sentinels resolved via resolve_config_value (R20b pattern) and let run_backtest.py/run_optimizer.py argparse defaults be None, retiring the test_walkforward.py:72 pin, or (b) delete backtest.max_concurrent_positions/optimize_patience from config.yaml+BacktestConfig if the 1-position harness is the intended research contract; in both cases make export.py write the resolved WalkForwardConfig and add a test that every BacktestConfig field shared with WalkForwardConfig either resolves from config or is documented as harness-only.

## hardcoded-research-05  [enforcement_gap | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** tests/test_r19_config_authority.py:77-80 (regex built from CONTRACT_DEFAULTS keys), :81 (SRC.rglob only), :187-190 (iterates CONTRACT_DEFAULTS); src/ait/strategies/iron_condor.py:144 (AIT_IRON_CONDOR_IV_FLOOR '15'), :189-190 (AIT_IC_DELTA_MIN '0.15' / AIT_IC_DELTA_MAX '0.30'), :210-211 (AIT_IC_EM_MIN '0.6' / AIT_IC_EM_MAX '1.3'); src/ait/risk/manager.py:435 (AIT_IV_RANK_CAP '85'), :470 (AIT_MAX_PER_EXPIRY '3'); run_backtest.py:145 (env.get('AIT_IRON_CONDOR_IV_FLOOR', '15')), :264 (--iv-floor default=15.0), :331 (os.environ['AIT_IRON_CONDOR_IV_FLOOR'] = ...)

**Claim:** R19c's policy ('config must be the ONLY place with values that manage trading') is enforced only over the 9 CONTRACT_DEFAULTS keys, so any live trading gate that reads os.environ directly with a private fallback and never joined the table is invisible to the policy tests. Seven such gates exist today, and two of them are the condor's primary construction filters (IV-rank floor 15, short-delta band 0.15-0.30) with the floor's default hand-copied into run_backtest.py. None has a config.yaml home; a mis-set .env value silently changes live strike selection with no divergence report.

**Evidence:** test_r19_config_authority.py:78-79: pattern = re.compile(r'environ\.get\(\s*"(' + '|'.join(k for k in CONTRACT_DEFAULTS if k.startswith('AIT_')) + r')"'); :81 'for path in SRC.rglob("*.py")'; :188-189 'missing = [k for k in CONTRACT_DEFAULTS if k not in self._EXEMPT and k not in CONFIG_BACKED]'. Enumerated raw reads (grep os.environ.get("AIT_ excluding runtime_env.py): iron_condor.py:144 AIT_IRON_CONDOR_IV_FLOOR default="15"; :189 AIT_IC_DELTA_MIN "0.15"; :190 AIT_IC_DELTA_MAX "0.30"; :210 AIT_IC_EM_MIN "0.6"; :211 AIT_IC_EM_MAX "1.3"; manager.py:435 '_iv_cap = float(os.environ.get("AIT_IV_RANK_CAP", "85"))'; manager.py:470 '_max_per_expiry = int(os.environ.get("AIT_MAX_PER_EXPIRY", "3"))'; portfolio.py:569 AIT_CREDIT_TOUCH_STOP "1" (this one is registered as live-bt-mirrors-03). config.yaml has no field for any of them. Register mirrors-06 covers IV-floor being a different QUANTITY from the engine's iv_floor; the missing config home / policy-scan gap is not registered.

**Trigger:** Any .env or shell that sets one of these keys (or a future knob added the same way): live gates move with no config_default_divergence entry and no test failure; test_every_trading_key_has_a_config_home stays green.

**Consequence:** The R19c policy tests stay green while the condor's primary construction filters (IV-rank floor 15, short-delta band 0.15-0.30, EM band 0.6-1.3) and two risk gates (IV-rank cap 85, 3 per expiry) live as code literals with env-only overrides and no config.yaml home or divergence reporting; an operator cannot see or set them in config, a stray env value moves live strike selection with no config_default_divergence entry, and any new os.environ.get("AIT_...", literal) in src/ or the repo-root scripts passes the enforcement scan.

**Suggested fix:** Promote the seven keys (plus the LIQ/ladder/timeout reads) into CONTRACT_DEFAULTS + CONFIG_BACKED with config.yaml homes (strategy.iron_condor.iv_rank_floor / delta_band / em_band; risk.iv_rank_cap / max_per_expiry; execution.entry_order_timeout / ladder_start) read via contract_float/flag; change test_no_raw_environ_reads to flag ANY os.environ.get("AIT_...") outside runtime_env.py against an explicit allow-list, and extend its walk to run_*.py and scripts/.

## hardcoded-research-06  [doc_vs_code | ops_reporting | live_path=False]

**Where:** run_backtest.py:175 ('"macro_flatten_enabled": os.environ.get("AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS", "0") == "1"' in the BACKTEST column), run_backtest.py:144 (live column from the applied-contract snapshot), src/ait/backtesting/engine.py:2491-2493 (engine actually reads contract_flag("AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS")); tests/test_r19_config_authority.py:81 (scan limited to src/)

**Claim:** The parity manifest's backtest column reports the macro flatten as whatever THIS process's environment says, with a fail-open '0' fallback, while the engine it describes resolves the flag through contract_flag (config.yaml risk.skip_macro_events: true). A bare `python run_backtest.py` therefore prints a false 'macro_flatten_enabled: live=True backtest=False' divergence for a run that actually mirrors live; under master.py (env pre-seeded by apply_runtime_env_defaults) it prints none. It is also a raw read of a contract key that R19's no-private-fallback scan cannot see because run_backtest.py is outside src/.

**Evidence:** Executed in a bare Python313 process: 'ENV AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS in this process: None', 'live.macro_flatten_enabled = True', 'backtest.macro_flatten_enabled = False', 'WARNINGS: ! macro_flatten_enabled: live=True backtest=False', 'engine reads contract_flag(AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS) = True'. logs/backtest_20260831_164000.log (master-spawned nightly): 'PARITY WARNINGS: none — backtest parameters match live.' engine.py:2492-2493: 'and contract_flag("AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS")'.

**Trigger:** Any run_backtest.py invocation from a shell/script that did not call apply_runtime_env_defaults() (operators, study scripts, CI).

**Consequence:** Any run_backtest.py invocation from a shell, study script or CI that did not call apply_runtime_env_defaults() prints a false 'macro_flatten_enabled: live=True backtest=False' parity warning (and serializes it into result.parity_manifest) for a run whose engine actually applies the flatten, so operators either distrust a correct run or learn to ignore parity warnings.

**Suggested fix:** run_backtest.py:175 -> contract_flag("AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS") (what the engine reads); extend the R19 no-raw-read scan to repo-root *.py and scripts/; add a test that build_parity_manifest(parse_args()) in a process with AIT_SKIP_MACRO_EVENTS unset yields no macro_flatten warning and backtest['macro_flatten_enabled'] == contract_flag(...).

## hardcoded-research-07  [logic | ops_reporting | live_path=False]

**Where:** src/ait/learning/adaptor.py:296 ('current = self._confidence_override or 0.65'), :298 ('if new_min <= current: return None'), :364 ('current = self._take_profit_overrides.get(strategy, 1.0)'), :328 ('current = self._stop_loss_override or 0.50'), :347 (trailing baseline 0.25), :278 (boost x1.25), :329 (tighten x0.85), :45-60 (AdaptationLimits literals never wired to settings.learning — R7 low item, still open); src/ait/learning/engine.py:41 (StrategyAdaptor(state, active=...) with limits=None); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:2513/2516 (overrides written to signal.trailing_stop_pct / signal.profit_target_pct — no reader in portfolio.py/executor.py); config.yaml risk.min_confidence: 0.50, learning.paper_trading_mode: true

**Claim:** The live adaptor's 'current value' baselines are stale literals from a 0.65-confidence / 100%-take-profit era: with risk.min_confidence at 0.50 a raise_min_confidence_to_0.60 insight is silently dropped (0.60 <= 0.65) and a raise to 0.70 is recorded as 'old 0.65'; an iron-condor take-profit insight is recorded against 'old 1.00' although the condor's short-side target is the 0.50/0.40/0.30/0.20 ladder. apply_insights runs these processors even when active=False, so learning_history and the Telegram learning summary carry fabricated old values today; at go-live (paper_trading_mode false) the dropped 0.60 raise becomes a live-behaviour bug.

**Evidence:** Executed (Python313, real StrategyAdaptor with an in-memory state): 'config risk.min_confidence = 0.5'; 'raise_min_confidence_to_0.60 with live min_confidence 0.50 -> None'; 'raise_min_confidence_to_0.70 -> ("0.65", "0.70")'; 'adjust_take_profit iron_condor 0.45 -> ("1.00", "0.45")'; 'live condor short TP at DTE 14 = (0.75, 0.4)'. adaptor.py:95-121 apply_insights has no self._active check. grep '.profit_target_pct|.trailing_stop_pct' in execution/: only portfolio.py:483 reading exit_config (override attributes unread). docs/GAP_AUDIT_R7.md:635 already notes the unplumbed LearningConfig knobs (values coincide today, executed: AdaptationLimits() == config 0.5/0.9/0.6/3).

**Trigger:** Any learning cycle producing a raise_min_confidence_to_<0.65> or adjust_take_profit insight (analyzer.py:224-241 / :431-440); live effect once learning.paper_trading_mode is set false at go-live.

**Consequence:** The first learning cycle whose confidence-band insight clears 0.6 (bands already firing at 0.2-0.27) will either be silently dropped (raise to 0.60 vs the fictitious 0.65 baseline while live is 0.50) or announced on Telegram and written to learning_history as 'risk.min_confidence: 0.65 -> 0.70' / 'exit.take_profit.iron_condor: 1.00 -> 0.45' — old values that match no live authority — with the word 'applied' while paper mode applies nothing; the override is nonetheless persisted to bot_state and goes live the moment paper_trading_mode is flipped at cutover.

**Suggested fix:** Resolve baselines from authorities (settings.risk.min_confidence, exit_policy.take_profit_targets(strategy, dte), exit.initial_stop_loss_pct / trailing_stop_pct) or pass them into StrategyAdaptor; build AdaptationLimits from settings.learning in LearningEngine; make apply_insights a no-op (or record 'observed_only' without _save_state and without the Telegram 'applied' message) when not active; or delete the adaptor per PLAN.md:518/524 wire-or-delete. Executing test: with risk.min_confidence=0.50, raise_min_confidence_to_0.60 must yield an Adaptation with old_value '0.50'; with active=False, apply_insights must not persist learning_adaptations.

## logic-entry-06  [enforcement_gap | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:1405-1413 ('prediction = self._predictor.predict(...)'; 'if prediction is None: log.warning("ml_prediction_none"); return'), :1384 (data-quality return); src/ait/ml/ensemble.py:146-156 (predict returns None when 'model not trained' or 'features_empty'); PLAN.md:1729-1735 (observe mode: 'The ML models keep predicting and logging; they no longer veto')

**Claim:** Observe mode still lets ML availability veto every condor: a direction-neutral structure cannot be built unless the DIRECTION ensemble returns a prediction, so a missing/corrupt ensemble.pkl, a failed retrain, or empty features silently stops all entries (warning log only, no page) — the same silent-sample-stop class W2 closed for the range gate (fail-direction-02), left open for the model the condor does not even use.

**Evidence:** orchestrator.py:1411-1413 returns from _scan_symbol before any chain is fetched or any condor built; ensemble.py:146 'log.warning("prediction_skipped", reason="model not trained", symbol=symbol)' then :147 'return None'. grep 'ml_prediction_none' in src/ait: only the one log site (orchestrator.py:1412) — no alert_gate/notification, unlike range_gate_unavailable (:1727-1733) and iv_rank_unavailable (:1346-1352). In observe mode the direction value is consumed only by the neutral_only/first-hour paths that already exempt condors (:1846-1857, :2386).

**Trigger:** Nightly retrain failure or artifact loss (documented incident class for range.pkl; R19 fixed the ensemble rollback that was dead) -> zero condor entries on every symbol until the next successful train, with only per-scan WARNING lines.

**Consequence:** A failed/absent nightly retrain, a clobbered ensemble.pkl (the same artifact-loss class documented for range.pkl), or a short history frame silently zeroes condor entries on every symbol until the next successful train, surfacing only as ml_prediction_none WARNING lines -- the 08-11 silent-full-stop signature that W2 closed for the range and VIX gates, still open for the model the condor does not consume in observe mode.

**Suggested fix:** When entry_gates_enabled is false, treat prediction None as direction NEUTRAL with the observe-mode neutral baseline and continue the condor path (log at WARNING), and in both modes count a per-symbol streak and page via _alert_gate('ml_prediction_unavailable', 3600) after >=3 consecutive None scans; make orchestrator.run act on a False from ensure_models_ready (page). Pin with an executing collect-mode test: predictor returning None must still yield a condor candidate in observe mode and must page after the streak.

Pin with an executing test that captures structlog events for iv_rank=5 and for a 1-strike chain.

## logic-exit-risk-04  [regression | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/bot/state.py:429-436 (close_trade enqueues every close into exit_outbox unconditionally); src/ait/execution/reconciler.py:855-859 (stale-pending sweep calls close_trade with realized_pnl=0.0, reason 'stale_pending_never_filled'); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:676-720 (_drain_exit_outbox: no exit_reason filter; :693-699 stats + record_trade_result; :712-714 Thompson; :715-720 ORPHAN CLOSE BOOKED page); src/ait/reporting/go_live.py:96-121 (NOT_REAL_CLOSE_PATTERNS / is_real_close); src/ait/risk/circuit_breaker.py:215-222 (pnl<0 increments, else resets the streak)

**Claim:** W1's exit outbox books NOT-REAL closes. The reconciler's stale-pending sweep (a routine event: 6 'stale_pending_never_filled' rows in the live DB) closes a never-filled entry with realized_pnl=0.0; close_trade enqueues it; 120 s later _drain_exit_outbox books it as a LOST trade in daily_stats, feeds $0 to CircuitBreaker.record_trade_result (which RESETS any real consecutive-loss streak), records a Thompson loss for iron_condor, and pages 'ORPHAN CLOSE BOOKED ... P&L $0.00 (stale_pending_never_filled)'. W3 rewired 'all 6 remaining consumers' to the one not-real-close authority; the W1 drain was not among them.

**Evidence:** Executed probe (real StateManager on a temp DB): INSERT pending trade -> `close_trade(..., realized_pnl=0.0, exit_reason_detailed='stale_pending_never_filled', from_statuses=(PENDING,))` -> `close_trade landed: True`; `pending_exit_bookings(0)` -> `[{'trade_id': 'T-PROBE-PEND', 'realized_pnl': 0.0, 'exit_reason': 'stale_pending_never_filled', ...}]`; `is_real_close('stale_pending_never_filled') = False`; real CircuitBreaker with two losses: `streak before phantom booking: 2` -> `record_trade_result(0.0)` -> `streak after phantom $0 booking: 0`; drain arithmetic `if pnl > 0: won else: lost` -> 'LOST (trades_lost += 1)'. orchestrator.py:676-720 contains no reference to is_real_close/not_real_close (grep: none in orchestrator.py). Live DB: 6 historical stale_pending_never_filled rows; exit_outbox currently empty (no sweep since the 08-31 restart), so the path is latent, not yet fired.

**Trigger:** The next entry order that never fills within the sweep age (any delayed-data day): daily_stats trades_lost +1, win-rate/EOD digest wrong, a real 2-loss streak zeroed, Thompson poisoned, and a false CRITICAL-style page. Same for reconciler 'reconciler_unknown_exit_needs_review' $0 sentinels (reconciler.py:43-46).

**Consequence:** The next never-filled-then-swept entry or reconciler $0 sentinel is booked as a LOST trade in daily_stats (EOD digest/win-rate wrong), records a PDT day trade that never happened, records a Thompson loss, fires a false 'ORPHAN CLOSE BOOKED' page, and - the one gating effect - zeroes a real consecutive-loss streak so the 3-loss pause cannot fire on the following real loss. The go-live verdict is unaffected (it reads trades via is_real_close).

**Suggested fix:** In _drain_exit_outbox claim the row but skip stats/breaker/PDT/Thompson/page when not is_real_close(row['exit_reason']) (log stale_exit_booking_not_real), or have close_trade skip the enqueue for NOT_REAL_CLOSE_EXIT_REASONS; executing test: a PENDING sweep close must leave daily_stats, the breaker streak, PDT and Thompson unchanged (fails pre-fix per the probe above).

## ops-substrate-03  [bug | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/orchestration/master.py:1405-1440 (cleanup_old_logs: `for d in [LOGS_DIR, REPORTS_DIR]` ... `if mtime < cutoff: f.unlink()`), master.py:1710-1713 (monthly_cleanup cron day=1 00:00, no catch-up), logs/orchestrator.log:47871 (`2026-08-01 00:00:00 cleanup_complete | removed=34`); reports/relationship_defect_hunt_20260825.md, reports/blindspot_composition_hunt_20260825.md (git-tracked, mtime Aug 25)

**Claim:** The monthly cleanup deletes EVERY file in reports/ older than 30 days by mtime with no name filter, so on its next run (2026-10-01 00:00 if the box is up) it silently deletes the two canonical audit registers PLAN.md points at, all five backtest_*.json snapshots and 36 daily_*.json reports; and because monthly_cleanup is the one scheduled job WITHOUT a startup catch-up, on a box that is powered off nightly it simply never runs (logs/ is at 785 MB with 566 MB eligible).

**Evidence:** Executed probe replicating cleanup_old_logs' exact selection (no deletion): 'today: reports 21 files (daily_20260702..0805.json), logs 21 files 45 MB'; '2026-10-01 00:00: reports 43 files incl. backtest_20260808..0831*.json, blindspot_composition_hunt_20260825.md, relationship_defect_hunt_20260825.md; logs 57 files 566 MB'. `git ls-files reports | grep .md` lists both registers (tracked, so recoverable by checkout, but the working-tree copies PLAN.md:1440/1456 cite as 'the register' vanish with no log line naming them). Only ONE cleanup_complete exists in the whole orchestrator.log (2026-08-01 00:00:00 removed=34); System log 1074 events show the operator powering off at 23:18 (08-31), 23:56 (09-01), 23:28 (09-02) etc., and Sep 1 00:00 was missed (box off 08-31 23:18 -> keeper relaunch 09-01 08:17:14; misfire_grace_time 3600s). du: logs 785M, of which range_floor_v2.log 97 MB, bot_stdout.log.1 91 MB, .2 76 MB, shadow_r3_gated.log 58 MB - one-off study logs nothing else prunes.

**Trigger:** First 1st-of-month 00:00-01:00 window the box is powered on with the master alive (next candidate 2026-10-01): the registers and every backtest/daily JSON older than 30 days are unlinked; `_backtest_catchup_due` then sees no backtest_*.json and re-runs the weekly backtest at the next boot. Conversely, every month the box is off at that hour the 30-day retention never applies and logs/ keeps growing (79 GB free on C: today).

**Consequence:** On its next execution (2026-10-01 00:00 if the box is up) cleanup_old_logs unlinks every top-level file in reports/ with mtime before 2026-09-01 — including both audit registers PLAN.md cites as 'the register', all five backtest_*.json and 36 daily_*.json — logging only an aggregate `removed=N`. Wiping backtest_*.json additionally makes _backtest_catchup_due return True, so the next boot schedules a spurious full weekly backtest.

**Suggested fix:** Restrict REPORTS_DIR pruning to generated artefacts (glob daily_*.json / backtest_*.json only, never *.md and never anything unrecognised), and log the deleted names, not just a count; give monthly_cleanup the same boot catch-up pattern the backup/retrain/digest/report/backtest jobs already use (run if no cleanup_complete this month). Executing test: a tmp reports dir holding a 60-day-old .md and a 60-day-old daily_*.json -> only the json is unlinked (fails pre-fix).

## params-ml-research-06  [parameter | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** config.yaml:197; src/ait/config/settings.py:352-369 (rationale: 'the 0.60 default sits above the shipped 0.50 with headroom'); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:1845-1857 (eff_conf), :2567-2570 (save_trade_context confidence=eff_conf), :1860-1870 (collect ranking eff_conf + 0.3*cw); src/ait/risk/position_sizer.py:98-101; data/ait_state.db trade_context (probe)

**Claim:** observe_mode_neutral_confidence 0.60 has no rationale beyond 'above 0.50 with headroom', yet it is not just a gate input: it becomes the persisted entry_confidence of every observe-mode condor (a constant 0.60, where prior trades recorded 0.54-0.99), it is the sizing input (position_sizer conf_adj = 0.5 + 0.5*((0.60-0.50)/0.50) = 0.60 -> every observe-mode condor sized at 60% of max once contracts > 1), and it makes the cross-symbol ranking degenerate to credit/width alone. The value was chosen for the gate but silently sets sizing and corrupts the confidence-band analytics.

**Evidence:** Probe: trade_context.entry_confidence for T-20260901-105959 (SPY) = 0.6 and T-20260901-110001 (QQQ) = 0.6; the four prior condors = 0.8978, 0.5449, 0.9915, 0.7412. orchestrator.py:1849-1850 `eff_conf = float(self._settings.ml.observe_mode_neutral_confidence)`; :2570 `confidence=confidence,` (the eff_conf passed to _try_execute at :1873). position_sizer.py:100-101 `min_conf = self._risk_config.min_confidence; conf_adj = 0.5 + 0.5 * ((confidence - min_conf) / (1.0 - min_conf))`. settings.py:366-368 is the entire justification for 0.60.

**Trigger:** Every observe-mode condor entry; sizing effect binds the day max_contracts_per_trade > 1 (Phase-2 scaling), analytics effect binds now (dashboard confidence-band analysis, drift/learning analyzers read entry_confidence).

**Consequence:** Every observe-mode condor is recorded with two contradictory confidences - trade_context.entry_confidence a constant 0.60 gate placeholder, trades.ml_confidence the raw directional 0.28/0.38 that the risk gate never actually evaluated - so the dashboard confidence-band analysis, the learning analyzer's band statistics and any meta-label/duckdb analysis of the observe-mode epoch measure a value that is neither the gate input nor a model output; sizing by conf_adj=0.60 becomes live only if max_contracts_per_trade is raised above 1 and adaptor steering only if paper_trading_mode is turned off.

**Suggested fix:** Persist the directional final_confidence, the neutral-baseline flag and (per finding 01) the range probability as separate fields, and write the same confidence to trades.ml_confidence and trade_context.entry_confidence; make PositionSizer use conf_adj=1.0 (or the range probability) for direction-neutral structures in observe mode; document in settings.py that observe_mode_neutral_confidence is also a sizing input and either justify 0.60 or set it to risk.min_confidence; add an executing test asserting the two persisted confidences for a gates-off condor are equal and are not the placeholder.

## params-ml-research-10  [logic | ops_reporting | live_path=False]

**Where:** src/ait/backtesting/walkforward.py:1486-1489 (`_evaluate_direction_model_oos(..., horizon_days=self._config.max_hold_days, ...)`), :2064-2071 (return_threshold_pct=0.02), :2106-2119 (labels = horizon_days forward return, +/-2% bands); src/ait/ml/ensemble.py:620-642 (_create_labels: 5-day forward return, +/-1% bands); config.yaml:154 (max_hold_days 30); src/ait/backtesting/engine.py:2472-2474

**Claim:** max_hold_days=30 has one remaining effect on the research record that is wrong: the direction-model OOS calibration is scored against 30-day forward returns with +/-2% bands while the model is trained on 5-day forward returns with +/-1% bands (PR#7 fixed only the range-model eval at :1474-1477, 'was max_hold_days (30)', and left the direction eval on max_hold_days). The dashboard/window JSON 'direction_predictor oos_scores' therefore describe a question the model never predicts. The baseline itself is otherwise inert for the live strategy: engine.py:2473 `held >= self._max_hold_days` can never fire for a 14-DTE condor closed at DTE<=5 (day 9).

**Evidence:** walkforward.py:1486-1489: `_dir_oos = self._evaluate_direction_model_oos(predictor, test_df, symbol, horizon_days=self._config.max_hold_days, ...)`; :2113-2119: `fwd_ret = (close[t + horizon_days] - close[t]) / close[t]; if fwd_ret > return_threshold_pct: ... elif fwd_ret < -return_threshold_pct:` with return_threshold_pct=0.02. ensemble.py:634-642: `fwd_return = close.pct_change(5).shift(-5)` ... `labels[fwd_return > 0.01] = 2; labels[fwd_return < -0.01] = 0`. PLAN.md:1341-1343 records the R21b fix for the range side only.

**Trigger:** Any walk-forward run with train_window_models=True (every ablation/gated study): direction calibration bins and per-member OOS accuracy are computed on 30d/2% labels for a 5d/1% model.

**Consequence:** Every walk-forward window with a trained direction model reports per-member and ensemble OOS calibration bins/AUROC scored on 30-day/+/-2% labels for a model trained on 5-day/+/-1% labels — the dashboard reliability charts and any reading of 'direction_predictor oos_scores' describe a question the model does not answer, and the eval is silently starved of ~40% of its labelable rows.

**Suggested fix:** Reuse DirectionPredictor._create_labels (or expose its horizon/threshold) inside _evaluate_direction_model_oos so OOS labels equal training labels; drop the max_hold_days coupling; add an executing test asserting the evaluator's y_class equals the trainer's labels on the same frame (fails pre-fix).

## regress-live-03  [enforcement_gap | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:1159 (only _drain_exit_outbox call site); 1094-1112 (breaker-tripped early return), 1149-1151 (last-15-min early return), 1019 (_trading_loop runs _trading_cycle only while phase == MARKET_OPEN); 657-665 (alert_fill_after_close read ONLY inside the drain); 684-690 (prior-day rows claimed and discarded); 3402 (_post_market reconcile -> close_trade -> outbox); src/ait/execution/executor.py:2486-2492, 2559-2566 (set_state overwrites the single flag)

**Claim:** The drain — and the CRITICAL 'fill landed on a TERMINAL row' page that lives inside it — is unreachable whenever _trading_cycle returns early (breaker tripped: rest of a daily-loss/MTM day; last 15 minutes of every session) and whenever the loop is outside MARKET_OPEN (the post-market reconcile at 3402 is where expiries/manual flattens/bot-down stop fills get closed). Rows created in those windows age past midnight and the next drain claims them and DISCARDS them (stale_exit_booking_skipped): those closes never reach PDT/Thompson/daily win-loss, and a fill-after-close event at 15:50 (the concurrency-1 sweep race, most likely near the close when entries are stale) pages at ~09:35 the next trading day while a real, unmanaged position sits at the broker overnight. A second such event before the drain overwrites the first (set_state, not append), so only one is ever paged.

**Evidence:** orchestrator.py:1094 `if self._circuit_breaker.is_tripped: ... return` and 1149 `if self._scheduler.should_avoid_new_trades(): ... return` both precede 1159 `await self._drain_exit_outbox()`; 1019 `while self._running and self._scheduler.get_current_phase() == TradingPhase.MARKET_OPEN:`; scheduler.py:119-120 `return 0 < remaining < 900`. Drain 686-690: `if exit_d != today: log.warning('stale_exit_booking_skipped', ...); continue` after the claim at 680. executor.py:2486-2489 `self._state.set_state('alert_fill_after_close', f'{pending.trade_id}|{signal.symbol}|{actual_price}')`. grep shows _drain_exit_outbox has exactly one caller (1159) and 'alert_fill_after_close' is read only at 657.

**Trigger:** Any post-market reconcile close (ITM expiry booked at 16:05; a stop that filled while the bot was down and is discovered by _post_market) -> never booked anywhere but trades.realized_pnl. A fill CAS-refused after 15:45 -> no page until next session. PLAN W1 claims 'EVERY close reaches breaker/stats/PDT/Thompson exactly once'; for these windows it reaches none of them.

**Consequence:** Post-market reconcile closes (ITM expiries, stops that filled while the bot was down), orphan closes in the last 15 minutes, and orphan closes on a daily-loss/MTM-halted day are claimed the next morning as 'stale_exit_booking_skipped' and never reach daily win/loss stats or Thompson — PLAN W1's 'every close reaches breaker/stats/PDT/Thompson exactly once' is false for those windows; and the CRITICAL 'fill landed on a TERMINAL trade row' page for a 15:50 event arrives ~09:35 next session, with any second such event silently overwritten.

**Suggested fix:** Call the flag-page + drain from _monitor_positions_fast (runs every 30s including tripped days and the last 15 min) and once at the end of _post_market after reconcile; key the flag per trade (alert_fill_after_close_<trade_id>) and page/delete every matching key via state_keys_like; for prior-day rows still record Thompson/PDT/trade counts (only the breaker's daily quantities are day-scoped). Executing tests: tripped breaker + backdated outbox row -> fast monitor books it; two flags set -> two pages.

## regress-live-09  [bug | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:669-717 (_drain_exit_outbox books row['realized_pnl'] with no commission true-up, no trueup_pending_ key, no update_trade_commission); contrast 750-787 (_process_completed_exits true-up / deferral) and 3378-3396 (post-market true-up iterates only trueup_pending_* keys)

**Claim:** A close whose booking reaches the breaker via the drain (executor callback lost to a crash, or the claim failure in regress-live-02) is booked at the outbox's realized_pnl, which for executor closes still embeds the flat $0.65/leg commission estimate; the drain neither trues it up nor registers trueup_pending_<id>, so the post-market pass never touches it. The R15 #8 defect (estimate never replaced by ledger truth) is re-opened for exactly the drain path W1 added.

**Evidence:** orchestrator.py:681 `pnl = float(row['realized_pnl'] or 0.0)` then 693-699 books it directly; no call to total_commission/count_executions/update_trade_realized_pnl/update_trade_commission in 669-717. _process_completed_exits 779-781 `self._state.set_state(f'trueup_pending_{trade_id}', ...)` has no counterpart in the drain; state.py:883 `SELECT key FROM bot_state WHERE key LIKE 'trueup_pending_%'` is the only post-market source.

**Trigger:** Bot dies between close_trade's commit and the callback (the concurrency-2 scenario W1 targets); on relaunch the drain books the estimate-based P&L and trades.realized_pnl keeps the $0.65-per-leg estimate forever while the real ledger shows ~$0.92/contract-leg (W5 measurement).

**Consequence:** When an executor close's booking is lost to a crash/restart and the drain books it on relaunch, trades.realized_pnl keeps the $0.65/leg estimate permanently (about $2.13/contract too generous on a condor per W5) and trades.commission stays 0.0, and the post-market true-up never visits it because no trueup_pending key exists — so the go-live PF (which sums trades.realized_pnl) and the commission column drift from the ledger for exactly the crash-recovery closes W1 was built to catch.

**Suggested fix:** Tag exit_outbox rows with their booking source / whether realized_pnl embeds the fill-time estimate (e.g. an `estimate_embedded` flag set by the executor's close paths, NULL for reconciler closes); in _drain_exit_outbox, for estimate-embedded rows run the same true-up block as _process_completed_exits (complete-ledger check, else set trueup_pending_<id>) and stamp update_trade_commission; add an executing test that closes a trade via the executor path, drops the callback, drains, and asserts realized_pnl/commission match the executions ledger or that trueup_pending_<id> exists.

## regress-research-01  [regression | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** src/ait/orchestration/master.py:830 (RangePredictor(threshold_pct=0.05, horizon_days=30) inside the retrain subprocess); master.py:1609-1611 (07:30 cron) and :1650-1660 (catch-up retrain on any post-07:35 boot); master.py:918 (.retrained marker) and :518-540 (marker restart); src/ait/ml/trainer.py:108-147 (check_range_spec logs only, never corrects); trainer.py:76-83 (needs_training -> True on spec_mismatch) and :512-523 (ensure_models_ready -> train_all_symbols); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:463 and :600 (ensure_models_ready at every boot); src/ait/ml/range_predictor.py:704-731 (load_models spec guard); src/ait/ml/range_spec.py:44-67; PLAN.md:1567/1639 (registered as a future flap) and PLAN.md:1682-1685 (attributes the horizon-9 rebuild to the overnight retrain)

**Claim:** The master.py:830 flap is not a future risk, it fires on EVERY retrain cycle today: the master's retrain subprocess rewrites models/range.pkl at horizon 30 each morning, and the only reason the live artifact reads horizon 9 is that the bot's spec-mismatch guard forces a FULL boot retrain (ensemble + range + vol_mag, all symbols) on the marker restart. That re-creates the every-boot retrain R9 removed (trainer.py:60-64 'a restart with fresh artifacts skips the boot retrain entirely'), trains every model family twice a day, and PLAN's 2026-09-01 statement that 'the overnight retrain rebuilt the range model at the corrected horizon 9' credits the wrong process.

**Evidence:** logs/retrain_20260901_081942.log, retrain_20260902_200717.log, retrain_20260903_081722.log each contain: 'range_train_spec_mismatch designed_horizon_days=30 designed_threshold=0.05 live_horizon_days=9' then 'range_models_saved horizon=30 path=...models\range.pkl'. logs/ait.log (UTC): 12:15:16Z range_models_loaded horizon=9 (boot) -> 12:19:24Z range_model_spec_mismatch loaded_horizon_days=30 version=range-v-20260903-081755 (the marker restart loaded the subprocess artifact) -> 12:19:34/45/57Z range_models_saved horizon=9 x3 -> 12:19:59Z training_complete version=v-20260903-081954; models/ensemble_v-20260903-081932/081943/081954.pkl written by the bot in the same window. Same pattern 09-02 at 00:11:04Z (loaded range-v-20260902-200753 horizon=30, rebuilt at 9 by 00:11:37Z). Python probe of models/range.pkl now: threshold 0.05 horizon 9 trained_at 2026-09-03T08:19:57 (the BOT's version, not the retrain subprocess's range-v-20260903-081755). master.py:830 still reads `range_pred = RangePredictor(threshold_pct=0.05, horizon_days=30)`.

**Trigger:** Every weekday: 07:30 cron (or the catch-up retrain 2 min after any post-07:35 supervisor boot) writes horizon 30; the next non-RTH health check restarts the bot, which rebuilds everything. If the box boots after 09:30 the catch-up retrain runs during RTH, the marker restart is deferred to after close, and any crash-relaunch that day (fatal.log shows one access-violation crash today at 08:38) loads the horizon-30 pickle and performs the full boot retrain with open positions before the trading loop starts (bot-day-04 window).

**Consequence:** Every trading morning the retrain subprocess writes a horizon-30 range.pkl that the marker restart then detects as a spec mismatch, forcing the bot into a full boot retrain of ensemble + range + vol_mag for all three symbols (~35s on 09-03/09-04) before reconcile or position monitoring — the every-boot retrain R9 removed is back, all model families are trained twice a day, the live artifact is always the bot's rebuild rather than the audited subprocess output, and on a late-boot day (catch-up retrain during RTH, 9 of the last 25 retrains) a crash-relaunch would perform that retrain with open positions unmanaged. trainer.check_range_spec is a log-only guard that cannot stop the divergent write.

**Suggested fix:** master.py:830-831: build the predictors from the authority (`from ait.ml.range_spec import live_range_spec; thr, hor = live_range_spec(settings); RangePredictor(threshold_pct=thr, horizon_days=hor)`); make ModelTrainer.check_range_spec() fail closed (raise / return False consumed by train_all_symbols so the subprocess exits non-zero and retrain_models() alerts) instead of logging; add an executing test that runs the retrain snippet's construction path and asserts the saved pickle horizon == live_range_spec()[1], plus a test that a marker restart with a fresh authority-spec artifact logs using_existing_models (R9 invariant).

## regress-research-03  [regression | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** status.py:97-104 (_count_event_today -> ops_health.count_events_today) and status.py:136-137 (called for 4 events inside get_status); src/ait/monitoring/ops_health.py:454-467 (count_events_today builds a NEW IncrementalEventCounter and force-refreshes every call) and :368-394 (_seed reads every rotated backup newer than local midnight, 64 MB cap EACH, plus the live tail); status_server.py:81 (setInterval(tick, 3000)) and :92 (each request calls get_status()); config.yaml:258-259 (20 MB x 10 backups); web_logs.py:64-69 (the module-level _COUNTER pattern status.py did not adopt)

**Claim:** W6 replaced status.py's bounded 2 MB tail with a one-shot counter that re-parses ALL of today's rotated ait.log backups on every call, and get_status() calls it once per event (4 events), so the 8503 status page re-reads and re-parses up to (rotations x 20 MB + live) x 4 on every 3-second poll — the same O(file)-per-poll class log-contracts-4 was opened for and web_logs.py was fixed for (it keeps one incremental counter per process).

**Evidence:** Measured on the repo's own files: `4 events x (4 backups + live) = 94 MB per file-set, 1.89s per get_status(); status_server polls every 3s` (ait.log.1-.4 are the 2026-09-01 rotations, 20,971,4xx bytes each). count_events_today: `counter = IncrementalEventCounter(path, events, ..., min_interval_s=0.0, ...); return counter.refresh(now=now, force=True)` — nothing is cached between calls; status.py:104 `return count_events_today(LOGS / 'ait.log', (event,)).counts.get(event, 0)` is evaluated four times per get_status(). Register log-contracts-1 documents 4 rotations in one RTH session; with the full 10-backup budget the per-poll cost scales to ~200 MB x 4.

**Trigger:** Any RTH afternoon after ait.log has rotated 2+ times that day while the 8503 status page (or any get_status() consumer) is open; the CLI `python status.py` pays the same cost once.

**Consequence:** On any RTH afternoon after ait.log has rotated 2+ times, every 3-second poll of the 8503 status page (and every `python status.py`) re-reads and re-parses ~100-200 MB x 4 of log — 1.9 s per get_status() measured at 4 rotations, scaling to the full 10-backup budget — on the same disk the bot is writing at ~10 MB/hour, with concurrent tabs multiplying it; the page and CLI become slow exactly during the busiest sessions, the class W6 was meant to close for web_logs.

**Suggested fix:** In status.py keep ONE module-level IncrementalEventCounter over all four events (mirror web_logs.py's _COUNTER, min_interval_s ~2 s) and read `.counts[ev]` from a single refresh per get_status(); keep count_events_today for the CLI one-shot only. Add an executing test that calls get_status() twice on a fixture log with rotations and asserts the second call's bytes_scanned is only the appended bytes.

## tests-honesty-01  [test_honesty | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py:47-80 (TestRiskManagerIsADirectionalGate; _manager 50-58; test_directional_confidence_is_rejected 66-75; test_neutral_baseline_passes_the_same_gate 77-80); tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py:29-44 (TestConfigContract, load_settings-only pins); src/ait/risk/manager.py:351-355 (the gate they name); PLAN.md:1740-1741; commit 43d0409 message

**Claim:** 5 of the '7 executing tests' shipped with observe mode never execute the changed code or the gate they are named for. test_directional_confidence_is_rejected builds a real RiskManager (_manager()) and discards it, then asserts `"confidence" in expected` where `expected` is an f-string it composed itself containing the literal word — it cannot fail. test_neutral_baseline_passes_the_same_gate is the same load_settings() pin as TestConfigContract:36-39 and never calls validate_trade. Only the two rig tests (106-130) touch the eff_conf hunk. The class docstring 'The real gate, executed — proving what each number does to a request' and PLAN's '7 executing tests (real risk-config contract...)' are false.

**Evidence:** tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py:68-75: `m = self._manager()` (unused) ... `expected = f"confidence {req.confidence:.2f} < min {cfg.min_confidence}"; assert "confidence" in expected`. Executed probe against the real RiskManager on the shipped config: conf=0.42 -> approved=False reason='confidence 0.42 < min 0.5'; conf=0.60 -> passed gate 2 then AttributeError '_open_positions' (gate 3). The premise holds, but no test in the file proves it.

**Trigger:** Any edit to manager.py:351-355 (e.g. a neutral-strategy exemption, a >= flip, or reading a different config field) or to the eff_conf hunk that still yields 0.60 for the single rig candidate: the file stays 7/7 green while observe mode silently blocks or admits condors.

**Consequence:** The two tests under `class TestRiskManagerIsADirectionalGate` — whose docstring reads "The real gate, executed — proving what each number does to a request" — never execute `RiskManager.validate_trade`. `test_directional_confidence_is_rejected` constructs `m = self._manager()` and discards it, then asserts `"confidence" in expected` where `expected` is an f-string the test itself composed containing that literal word: it is a tautology that cannot fail for any state of manager.py. Its other line, `assert req.confidence < cfg.min_confidence`, is a verbatim duplicate of TestConfigContract:41-44. `test_neutral_baseline_passes_the_same_gate` is a verbatim duplicate of TestConfigContract:36-39 and mentions no manager at all. Consequence: any edit to the min_confidence gate (a neutral-strategy exemption, a `>=` flip, reading a different config field) leaves this file 7/7 green while observe mode silently blocks or admits condors, and PLAN.md:1740-1741 plus commit 43d0409 continue to advertise "7 executing tests (real risk-config contract, real orchestrator scan...)" as the proof of record for the 2026-09-01 observe-mode decision.

**Suggested fix:** Make both tests await the real gate: stub `_open_positions`/state so `validate_trade` is reachable, then assert `approved is False` with reason `confidence 0.42 < min 0.5` at 0.42 and a non-confidence outcome at 0.60. Delete the `"confidence" in expected` tautology and the unused `_manager()` call. Correct PLAN.md:1740 and the commit note to "2 executing gate tests + 3 config pins + 2 rig tests".

## tests-honesty-02  [test_honesty | ops_reporting | live_path=True]

**Where:** tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py:113-114 and 126-127 (`if not confs: pytest.skip(...)`); tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py:94-104 (_collect); src/ait/bot/orchestrator.py:1297 (_scan_symbol catches its own exceptions), 1719-1731 (range-gate drop filters `signals`)

**Claim:** The only two observe-mode tests that execute the fix turn an empty candidate list into SKIP, so the regression shape 'condors dropped / never reach risk validation' — the exact 2026-09-01 live state (16 all_gates_passed, 0 orders) — reports as green ('Suite N green' counts skips). PLAN's pre-fix proof only exercised the other shape (directional fallback != 0.60).

**Evidence:** Executed probe on the smoke rig: `candidates collected: 1 [('iron_condor', 0.6)]` — the assert path hangs on exactly one synthetic-chain signal. With `_range_predictor` untrained and gates ON (test 2's mode), orchestrator.py:1719-1731 filters every RANGE_GATED signal and `_collect` returns [] -> pytest.skip('no direction-neutral candidate in the sandbox chain') instead of a failure. Same for any exception inside _scan_symbol (swallowed) or a chain change that yields no condor.

**Trigger:** Run tests/test_observe_mode_neutral_confidence.py after any change that stops the sandbox chain producing an iron_condor, or with the range stub untrained: 2 skipped, 0 failed.

**Consequence:** Both rig tests convert an empty candidate list into `pytest.skip`, so the exact 2026-09-01 live regression shape — every iron condor generated then dropped before ranking, 16 `all_gates_passed`, 0 orders — reports as green (a skipped test is not a failure, and "Suite N green" counts skips). The pre-fix proof PLAN records only exercised the other shape (a directional fallback != 0.60), so the shape that actually took the book flat has no failing-test coverage at all.

**Suggested fix:** Replace both `pytest.skip` calls with `assert confs, 'no direction-neutral candidate reached collect — the scan dropped every condor'`. A rig test that skips on empty output cannot distinguish 'the fix works' from 'nothing reached the fix'.

(b) The range-gate drop at 1719-1731 filters `signals` and continues, so `scan_symbol_timing` (1815) — the marker both the smoke test and scripts/smoke_deploy.py treat as 'completed a full chain/strategy pass' — is still logged when every candidate was dropped; neither asserts a candidate reached ranking. A build in which every iron-condor entry is refused fail-closed passes 'SMOKE PASS' with 'cycle+monitor executed clean'. (c) The allow-list still carries `daily_iv_persist_failed` although the R18 fix landed, so a regression of the self-healing IV store (feeds _estimate_iv_rank -> both IV gates) is swallowed by both the suite smoke and the deploy gate.

**Evidence:** Executed probe on build_smoke_orchestrator(): `events containing 'daily_iv': ['daily_iv_persisted']`, `failures() (after allow-list): []`; `spy would flag 'iv_rank_unavailable_entry_skipped': False`, 'range_gate_unavailable_entries_blocked': False, 'combo_nbbo_unavailable_entry_refused': False, 'working_order_check_disconnected_entry_refused': False, 'pdt_state_unreadable_fail_closed': False, 'pdt_calendar_empty_fail_closed': False, 'pdt_calendar_unavailable_fail_closed': False, 'restricted_list_unreadable_fail_closed': False, 'entries_halted_restricted_list_unreadable': False, 'order_refused_market_closed': False (only cooldown_check_failed_entry_refused / market_hours_guard_failed / touch_stop_evaluation_failed match). tests/test_w2_orchestrator_gates.py:553-562 already shows `collected == []` on the same rig with assert_clean untouched.

**Trigger:** Any deploy in which the armed gates refuse every entry (the 09-01 shape), or any regression that re-raises inside _persist_daily_iv.

**Consequence:** scripts/smoke_deploy.py can print "SMOKE PASS ... cycle+monitor executed clean" for a build in which the armed gates refuse every entry the cycle generated. Its three stage markers include `scan_symbol_timing`, which orchestrator.py:1815 logs AFTER the range-gate drop at 1719-1731; its "reached risk validation" check is satisfied by `_drive`'s hand-built `iron_condor_signal()` passed straight into `_try_execute`, bypassing the scan's own candidates entirely. Separately, `daily_iv_persist_failed` is still in the smoke's allow-list although the R18 fix it documents has landed, so a regression of the self-healing IV store (which feeds `_estimate_iv_rank` and both IV gates) is swallowed by the suite smoke and the deploy gate alike.

**Suggested fix:** In scripts/smoke_deploy.py, drive one `_scan_symbol(..., collect=candidates)` and raise unless `candidates` is non-empty (the marker `scan_symbol_timing` cannot distinguish a full pass from an all-refused pass). Delete `daily_iv_persist_failed` from `_ALLOWED_FAILURE_EVENTS` per its own instruction. Optionally add an explicit assertion in the smoke that no `*_refused` / `*_blocked` / `fail_closed` event fired, rather than widening `_FAILURE_TOKENS` (which would change the spy's stated meaning).

**Claim:** 40 inspect.getsource string assertions remain in 11 files despite the testing law, and three are the ONLY coverage of a live behaviour: (i) BotManager.health_check's post-market restart deferral (`fresh_models_restart_deferred_post_market`, marker must survive) is never executed — the only executing health_check tests pin `_in_post_market_window` to False, so the R16 restart-loop guard is proven by substring only; (ii) `assert "INERT" in src.upper()` is satisfied by comments and by the method name `_warn_delta_rule_inert` — it cannot fail short of deleting a comment; (iii) the `_process_completed_exits` test pins a hand-copied literal tuple ('iron_condor','short_strangle','long_straddle') instead of testing commission true-up scope, enshrining exactly the set-membership copy class the R22 register hunts.

Pin it too' (pinned False in every executing test); tests/test_r16_tail_fixes.py:731-732 `deferred = src.split("fresh_models_restart_deferred_post_market")[0]; assert deferred.count("marker.unlink()") == 0`.

A refactor that unlinks the retrain marker before the window check, or drops the branch, fails no test — and reintroduces the exact R16 incident it exists to prevent: the replacement bot starts inside the POST_MARKET phase and re-runs `_post_market`, producing a second EOD RECON, a second Telegram daily summary, a second reconcile and a second learning cycle (~80s after the first; observed on 5 of 8 sessions 07-28 through 08-05).
